<script>
$(".suitSelect").change(function(){
  $('.halloweenSuitUpConfirm').removeClass('hidden');
  if($(this).val()=="spermy"){
    $(".spermy-selects").removeClass("hidden"),$(".weapons-selects").addClass("hidden"),$(".spidey-selects").addClass("hidden")
  }else if($(this).val()=="weapons"){
    $(".spermy-selects").addClass("hidden"),$(".weapons-selects").removeClass("hidden"),$(".spidey-selects").addClass("hidden")
  }else if($(this).val()=="spidey"){
    $(".spermy-selects").addClass("hidden"),$(".weapons-selects").addClass("hidden"),$(".spidey-selects").removeClass("hidden")
  }
});
$(".halloweenSuitUpConfirm").click(function() {
  $(".halloweenSuitUpError").addClass("hidden"),$(".halloweenBlazerUpError").addClass("hidden");
  if($(".suitSelect").val()=="spermy"){
    var blz = parseInt( $( ".spermy-selects .blazerUpSelect" ).val() );
    var pnt = parseInt( $( ".spermy-selects .pantsUpSelect" ).val() );
    var tie = parseInt( $( ".spermy-selects .tieUpSelect" ).val() );
  }else if($(".suitSelect").val()=="weapons"){
    var blz = parseInt( $( ".weapons-selects .blazerUpSelect" ).val() );
    var pnt = parseInt( $( ".weapons-selects .pantsUpSelect" ).val() );
    var tie = parseInt( $( ".weapons-selects .tieUpSelect" ).val() );
  }else if($(".suitSelect").val()=="spidey"){
    var blz = parseInt( $( ".spidey-selects .blazerUpSelect" ).val() );
    var pnt = parseInt( $( ".spidey-selects .pantsUpSelect" ).val() );
    var tie = parseInt( $( ".spidey-selects .tieUpSelect" ).val() );
  }

  if(!blz){
    $(".halloweenBlazerUpError").removeClass("hidden"),
    $(".halloweenSuitUp").modal("show");
  }else{
    CartJS.addItem(blz, 1, {}, {
      "success": function(data, textStatus, jqXHR){
        $(".halloweenSuitUpError").addClass("hidden");
        $("#halloweenSuitUp").modal("hide");
      },
      "error": function(jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown){
        $(".halloweenSuitUpError").removeClass("hidden");
      }
    });
    if(pnt){
      CartJS.addItem(pnt,1);
    }
    if(tie){
      CartJS.addItem(tie,1);
    }
  }
});
$(".usaUpConfirm").click(function() {
  var res = parseInt( $(this).siblings( ".usaUpSelect" ).val() );
  CartJS.addItem(res, 1, {}, {
    "success": function(data, textStatus, jqXHR){
      $(".usaUpError").addClass("hidden");
      $("#usaUp").modal("hide");
    },
    "error": function(jqXHR, textStatus, errorThrown){
      $(".usaUpError").removeClass("hidden");
    }
  });
});
$(".swimUpConfirm").click(function() {
  CartJS.addItem(48146670415, 1, {}, {
    "success": function(data, textStatus, jqXHR){
      $("#swimUp").modal("hide");
    }
  });
});

<script>
  // Total seconds to wait
  var seconds = 5;
  var timer = 0;

  function countdown() {
      seconds = seconds - 1;
      if (seconds < 0) {
          // Chnage your redirection link here
          window.location = "https://www.tellumandchop.com/pages/ncaa-team-apparel-collection";
      } else {
          // Update remaining seconds
          document.getElementById("countdown").innerHTML = seconds;
          // Count down using javascript
          timer = window.setTimeout("countdown()", 1000);
      }
  }

  // Run countdown function
  $('#ncaaModal').on('shown.bs.modal', function (e) {
    countdown();
  })
  document.getElementById('stopNcaaTimer').addEventListener('click', function() {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    $("#ncaaModal").modal('hide');
    seconds = 5;
  });
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    
    var $cquery = jQuery.noConflict(true);
    
    // make sure redirect_to in register and login don't go to checkout if cart contains type 'Conjured Subscription'
    $cquery(document).ready(function() {
        
    });

    // $('.additional-checkout-button').remove();

    var checkout_buttons = [
        '[name="checkout"]',
        '[href="/checkout"]',
        'form[action="/checkout"] input[type="submit"]',
        'form[action="/checkout"] button[type="submit"]',
        '.checkout_button'
    ];
    var checkout_forms = [
        'form[action="/checkout"]',
        'form[action="/cart"]'
    ];
    $cquery(document).on('click', checkout_buttons.join(', '), function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        redirectToConjured();
    });
    $cquery(document).on('submit', checkout_forms.join(', '), function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        redirectToConjured();
    });

    function redirectToConjured() {

        
        
        
            window.location.href='/checkout';

        
    }
</script>

Shopify.AjaxifyCart = (function($) {
  var _config = {
    addToCartBtnLabel:'Add To Cart',
    addedToCartBtnLabel:'It\'s In.',
    addingToCartBtnLabel:'Adding...',
    soldOutBtnLabel:'Sold Out',
    howLongTillBtnReturnsToNormal:5000,
    cartCountSelector:'.cart, #cart-count a:first, #gocart p a, #cart .checkout em, .item-count',
    cartTotalSelector:'#cart-price',
    feedbackPosition:'nextButton',
    addToCartBtnSelector:'[type="submit"]',
    addToCartFormSelector:'form[action="/cart/add"]',
    shopifyAjaxAddURL:'/cart/add.js',
